# All settlements

[← wiki index](/Tarnholm/ris-wiki/wiki/README) · [all regions](/Tarnholm/ris-wiki/wiki/regions) · [settlement sizes](/Tarnholm/ris-wiki/wiki/sizes) · [cultures](/Tarnholm/ris-wiki/wiki/cultures) · [beliefs](/Tarnholm/ris-wiki/wiki/religions)

1,312 settlements, one per region. 220 of them are a faction's capital.
Search finds a city by name here; the region it sits in is the land around it, with the terrain,
fertility and trade goods.

Size is a rung on a ladder of 6, and it decides what a settlement can build and raise —
each one links to [what that size does](/Tarnholm/ris-wiki/wiki/sizes).
